- **algorithmic-awareness**: Understanding how algorithmic systems shape what users see, know, and do -- from recommendation feeds to search ranking to credit scoring to hiring software. Covers the mechanics of recommendation systems, algorithmic bias and its sources, personalization's effects on information diets, opacity and accountability, AI limitations (hallucination, confident wrongness), and the human-in-the-loop question. Use when a learner needs to think critically about why particular content reached them. ([Tibsfox/gsd-skill-creator](https://github.com/Tibsfox/gsd-skill-creator/tree/HEAD/examples/skills/digital-literacy/algorithmic-awareness) · ★71)

- **algorithmic-complexity**: Reason about how running time and memory grow with input size, and know when the asymptotic answer is the wrong one. Use when choosing an approach, reviewing code that loops over data, or explaining why something slows at scale. ([Amey-Thakur/AI-SKILLS](https://github.com/Amey-Thakur/AI-SKILLS/tree/HEAD/skills/computer-engineering/algorithmic-complexity) · ★7)
- **algorithmic-complexity-analyzer**: Rigorous, language-independent algorithmic complexity analysis (Big-O time and space complexity). Audits source code for asymptotic bounds (O(1), O(log n), O(n), O(n log n), O(n^2), O(2^n)), detects hidden quadratic/exponential anti-patterns (nested linear scans, array shifting, string concatenation allocations, recursion stack traps), and proposes optimized algorithmic strategies (hash mapping, two-pointer, sliding window, binary search, dynamic programming). Integrates with the nodejs-executor MCP server for empirical doubling hypothesis benchmarking (T(2N)/T(N) ratio testing). Use when ask… ([nainishshafi/developer-productivity-skills](https://github.com/nainishshafi/developer-productivity-skills/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/algorithmic-complexity-analyzer) · ★1)
- **algorithmic-complexity-attacks**: This skill should be used when the user asks "is this regex safe", "what is ReDoS", "why does CPU spike on one request", "how do I safely accept uploads or XML", "is this parser safe against untrusted input", or is reviewing a regex over user input, handling compressed files, or responding to a ReDoS advisory. ([auralshin/coding-skills](https://github.com/auralshin/coding-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/algorithmic-complexity-attacks))
- **algorithmic-complexity-review**: Algorithmic complexity (Big-O) review — finding nested loops, N+1 queries, exponential recursion, quadratic string builds, and other accidental complexity blowups.
